# Taxonomy proposal: bootstrap ingest

Ticket 06. Generated 2026-11-23 from `taxonomy-classification.jsonl` in this folder (one classified line per Source). **Status: awaiting maintainer approval.**

## Summary

- Sources classified: 638: 72 manufacturer (searspartsdirect.com), 566 expert-blog (poweredoutdoors.com), no forum Sources.
- To ingest: 606. To skip: 32.
- Proposed pages: 26 Problems, 110 Causes, 44 Procedures, 16 Guides. 16 already exist (marked **(exists)**): the fill pass updates them and never recreates them.
- **Accuracy caveat.** Classification was done by 8 parallel agents, mostly from titles and H2/H3 headings, not full reads. Cause-to-Problem links count only focused Sources (1-2 Problems); roundups still map to every page they touch. Problem and Guide mappings are reliable. Cause lists on multi-problem roundups ("13 Common X Problems") were partly filled from a standard Cause set per symptom, and Procedure mappings were largely inferred from Causes. The fill pass must read each Source in full and cite only what it says; a mapping here that turns out empty is dropped. Counts here are upper bounds, not the "How common" ranking.

## Decisions for the maintainer

1. **Sibling splits.** engine-wont-crank, engine-cranks-but-wont-start, engine-starts-then-dies, engine-dies-when-hot, engine-hard-to-start-when-cold and engine-only-runs-with-choke-on are separate Problems. There is no generic "won't start" page: generic won't-start Sources feed both crank siblings (and battery-mower-wont-start for electric mowers).
2. **battery-mower-wont-start** is split by Power source, an exception to "Power source is a tag", because its Causes share nothing with gas engines. Alternative: tag only, folded into the crank siblings.
3. **"Isn't getting gas" Sources** get no Problem of their own; they feed the fuel-supply Causes of cranks-but-won't-start and starts-then-dies, as tickets 01-04 already did.
4. **Thin Problems** (1-2 Sources): engine-backfires, mower-is-leaking-oil, loose-steering, mower-creeps-in-neutral, flat-tire. Keep, or fold into neighbours?
5. **Skip calls.** Zero-turns-on-hills is un-skipped into the zero-turn-safety Guide. The cordless-mower buying guide stays skipped. lawn-mower-replacement is kept as a Guide-only Source.
6. **Diesel Causes** (bad-glow-plugs, air-in-fuel-system) come from one Source and are kept as Causes of engine-cranks-but-wont-start.

## Clusters for the fill pass

One cluster per Problem: the Problem, the Causes it owns (Causes whose most frequent Problem it is) and the Procedures that fix those Causes. Each Cause and Procedure has exactly one owning cluster. "Shared" lists Causes owned by another cluster that this Problem also links to: those clusters must run first, and this cluster updates the shared page rather than creating it. Order: the existing cranks-but-won't-start cluster first, then largest first. Guides are the final cluster.

### engine-wont-crank

- Aliases: "won't turn over", "clicks but won't start", "no click when I turn the key", "nothing happens when I turn the key"
- Split: sibling of engine-cranks-but-wont-start and engine-starts-then-dies: electrical/starter Causes vs fuel/spark/air Causes
- Causes (28, Source count in brackets): weak-or-dead-battery (92), bad-starter-solenoid (88), bad-ignition-switch (74), loose-or-corroded-battery-cables (73), bad-safety-switch (73), faulty-charging-system (54), dirty-carburetor (52), old-fuel (52), clogged-fuel-line (52), fouled-spark-plug (52), clogged-fuel-filter (51), clogged-air-filter (51), plugged-fuel-cap-vent (49), bad-fuel-pump (46), bad-starter-motor (44), blown-fuse (42), bad-ignition-coil (39), empty-fuel-tank (36), incorrect-starting-procedure (35), bad-wiring-or-ground (20), bad-recoil-starter (20), wrong-choke-setting (13), wrong-fuel (8), sheared-flywheel-key (2), low-compression (2), wet-electrical-components (2), hydrolocked-engine (1), seized-engine (1)
